COST CONVENTION  2 bp PER SIDE on traded notional (primary) — a round trip costs 4 bp. replay.py charges per fill (= per side, the same as here); nulltest.py charges ONCE per round trip (half this for the same number). Convert before comparing.
CASH, FINANCING  1.50%/yr constant; borrowed money pays cash + 1.50%/yr; idle cash and borrowed money accrued by calendar days, (1+y)^(D/365) - 1 on each overnight leg (D = days since the previous close; the round-1 end-of-day convention)
INSTRUMENT       exposure e on the underlying itself; the borrowed (e-1)+ pays cash + spread and idle (1-e)+ earns cash, both on the overnight leg. A model of a frictionless margin loan: no maintenance calls, no whole-share rounding, no expense ratio
PRICES           closes only: each session is one close-to-close step and no open is read; a payout added back makes the ex-date's return (close + D) / close[t-1] - 1
DRIFT            shares are held between declared changes, as a margin account behaves: an auction trades only when the declared exposure changes, and the exposure drifts with the price in between. Bar (A) holds shares the same way and is reset to its target at every auction where the rule trades, so both books trade on the same days
DIVIDENDS        total-return THROUGH 2025-03-21 (each ex-date's dividend added back on that day's OVERNIGHT leg), price-only AFTER it: a later ex-date's drop lands in an overnight leg with no dividend credited, and a book holding more exposure that night is charged more of it (r1-quartermaster §5; r2-bench §1a); in this closes-only run each session is one close-to-close step, so a payout already in the prices lands in its ex-date's session
                 added back, every book alike (as PRICES says): 2025-06-20 1.7611, 2025-09-19 1.8311, 2025-12-19 1.9934, 2026-03-20 1.797, 2026-06-18 1.90352 (from spy-distributions-2025-2026.csv, sha256 780a360f01fa86b4…)
INFORMATION      open auction of t sees bars through close[t-1]; close auction of t sees bars through close[t-1] plus open[t]; calendar = the SCHEDULED NYSE calendar (barqc), so an unscheduled closure is not known to the rule
WINDOW           from the close of 2005-03-24 to the close of 2026-08-25: 5387 sessions, 10774 legs, 257 cycles
                 halves split by session at 2015-12-07 (first half = 2694 sessions); crisis spans removed for G6: 2008-09-02..2009-06-30, 2020-02-18..2020-04-30
BAR (A)          constant exposure 1.333395 = the rule's mean declared exposure over its 10774 scored legs; reset at the 513 auctions where the rule trades; weighted by the calendar days of each overnight leg the mean exposure would be 1.338446
